Shared qualifications:

- C1: source alloy formulas are atomic percentages, whereas CRA element columns are wt%. For a complete ternary formula, wt%_i = 100*x_i*M_i / sum(x_j*M_j). Atomic weights from CIAAW Abridged Standard Atomic Weights 2024 (https://ciaaw.org/abridged-atomic-weights.htm): Al26.982, Co58.933, Ce140.12, Ni58.693, Y88.906, Fe55.845, Gd157.25. Only relevant elements enter each explicitly stated ternary; no omitted alloy component is filled from CRA.
- C2: V(SCE)*1000=mV(SCE). Table 1 mean+/- values are at99% confidence; corresponding CRA Min/Max cells equal the scaled mean-minus/plus uncertainty endpoints. They are not observed population minima/maxima. Individual plotted readings can lie outside that interval without contradicting the mean.
- Table 1 (PDF p.5) identifies free-standing pure Al (~10^-1 cm2), microelectrode pure Al (~10^-4 cm2), and microelectrode alloy/phase summaries. Methods distinguish exposed cross-section micro-band electrodes from large-area free-standing ribbons, with different surface preparation (PDF pp.3-4). Generic CRA flat-cell text alone does not erase the area distinction.
- Supported shared rows represent Table 1 population summaries together with registered distribution members and, for701, a separate median. Members are not asserted equal to the mean; potential_agreement is not_comparable for individual-versus-summary roles. No new mean, pointwise merge across figures, replicate identity or independence is asserted. Fig.7 survival distributions have conflicting tails/ranks compared with cumulative plots; these are retained rather than reconciled.
- Registry obs_129-143 call the Fig.8(b) open triangles free-standing10^-1 cm2. The actual enlarged caption and area arrow identify triangles as mb10^-4 cm2; diamonds denote free-standing alloys. The original registry description remains unchanged. Source marker/caption evidence supports these IDs as microelectrode population members of703, with the area conflict explicit.
- Registry obs_125-128 remain partial with the original limitation: diamond/triangle overlap prevents secure free-standing membership recovery. The first two registered markers are visibly separated free-standing diamonds and lack a CRA free-standing-alloy counterpart. At the crowded upper markers127/128, possible microelectrode membership of702 remains candidate-only; neither the registry status nor the plotted reading is repaired.
- CRA707 uses Al90/Fe5/Gd5 in wt% columns, apparently retaining the source atomic-percent numbers. C1 instead gives Al69.50432/Fe7.99189/Gd22.50380 wt%. Its crystalline state,550 C/1 h context (CRA says551 C), and complete Table 1 potential/uncertainty identify the proposed row despite these explicit conflicts.
- Table 1 pure-Al micro mean-0.376 V conflicts with the p.7 text mean-0.350 V; obs_003 retains both and is discrepant. The separate p.5 median-0.348 V is not silently substituted. The fully crystalline Al-Ni-Y Table 1 value-0.143 V is retained despite the narrative loss of enhanced resistance. Source pH is described as near neutral; numerical CRA pH7.0 and test25 C are not independently inferred from the inspected method passages.
